- **playwright-locator-fill-element-is-not-2bf5919123602a00**: Fixes the Playwright "locator.fill: Element is not visible" flake that shows up when a page animation has not finished in CI. It covers waiting for the element to settle, disabling animations for test runs, and picking locators that do not fight transitions. Use when fill or click actions flake in CI but pass locally because the CI machine renders slower. Not for elements that are never visible (that is a real UI bug), and not for strict-mode violations from duplicate elements. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/playwright-locator-fill-element-is-not-2bf5919123602a00))

- **playwright-locator-scrollintoviewifnee-8b2d1c9f33800a53**: Fixes Playwright "locator.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded: Element is not visible" that only fails on CI's smaller viewport.
